- **classifier-statistics**: Statistical rules for Chirping's species posterior (MODEL, logLik, posterior, makePrior, regionPrior, gbifReweight in index.html) and for evaluating it (tests/accuracy-benchmark.mjs). Use before changing likelihood terms, priors, hyper-parameters or confidence display, before claiming any accuracy number, and when designing or reading a benchmark.
